- **Buyer**, ôl-in, pl. ôl-u.

### C

- **Calabash, a** (*for water*), sĕt, pl. sĕt-on.
  (*for milk*), muk-a, pl. muk-on.
  *Half a calabash, used as a cup, &c.*, kurit, pl. kurit-ai.
  *To drink from calabash*, r. nyiput, pr. o-nyiput-an, -ei, p.p. ko-nyiput-an.
  *Stick for cleaning calabash*, sōs-ion, pl. sōs.
  *Calabash fruit*, (coll.) tagi-nya-ril; tagi-nya-ril-ech.
  *Original contents of the calabash fruit*, tandar (coll.).
  *Calabash tree*, silangw-a.
- **Calf** (*very small*), maugh, pl. mōgh.
  (*half grown*), toñgmw-a, pl. toñgmw-oi.
- **Call, to**: r. kur, pr. o-kur-an, -ei, p.p. ko-kur-an.
- **Call out, to**: r. wut, pr. o-wut-an, -ei, p.p. ko-wut-an; (ii) r. tegh.
- **Camel**, tamas,¹ pl. tamas-ai.
- **Camp, a**, (ka-)ruw-on,² pl. ruwon-tin; r. ruw, *sleep*.
- **Can**, r. mwuch. *Vide* **Be able**.
- **Cap** (*head-dress*), alim, pl. alim-ai.
- **Cape** (*of skins*), kalach-a, pl. kalach-is.
- **Caravan**: *of one man and his goods*, üun, pl. üun-tin.
  *Of many men and their goods*, ki-wu-wu pich.
- **Caravan porter** (*vide* **Servant**), metuar-in, pl. metuar.
- **Carcase**, por-to, pur, pl. por-w-ei.
- **Care, to**: r. ros, pr. o-ros-an, -ei, p.p. kô-ros-an.
  *I don't care; No matter!* me kue!
- **Carry, to**: r. p. *Vide* **To bear**.
- **Castor-oil plant**, men-wa, pl. măn.

> ¹ r. *tamas*, be drunk, giddy, or sick. Possibly there is some allusion to the disagreeable motion of the camel (?).
> ² Ka-ruw-on is the name of a camp near Eñ-ginyañg.

- **Castrate, to**: r. lat, pr. o-let-an, -ei, p.p. ka-lat-an.

- **Catch, to**: r. nam, pr. o-nem-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nam-an.
  (*in a trap*): r. mar, pr. o-mer-an, -ei (merap), p.p. ka-mar-an.

- **Cattle-fold**, kā-tīch, pl. kiston-i-tīch.
- **Cave**, kepen, pl. kepen-is: kepen-ot.

  *To cease talking*: r. sis. *Vide* **Be silent**.
- **Ceiling**, *use adv.* yĭm, *above*.
- **Centipede**, hil-ian, pl. hil.
- **Chaff**, mŭr (coll.).

- **Chair**, ngechĕr, pl. ngechēr.
- **Chameleon**, nyĕrĕt, pl. nyīrīt.
- **Change for, to**: r. wel, pr. wel-j(ch)in-an, -ei, p.p. ka-wel-j(ch)-an.
- **Change, to** (*trans.*): r. wal, pr. o-wel-an, -ei, p.p. ka-wal-an.
- **Charcoal**, sutoh (coll.); (ii) warach. *Vide* **Alter**.
- **Charm.** *Vide* **Amulet**.

- **Chase away, to**: r. it, pr. o-it-oi-an, pp. kô-it-i-an.
- **Chatter, to**: r. kôr, pr. o-kôr-an, -ei, p.p. kô-kôr-an.

- **Cheat, to**: r. les, pr. o-les-an, -ei, p.p. ka-les-an.
- **Cheek, a**, matainy, pl. matŏñg.
- **Chest.** *Vide* **Breast**.

  *To chop fine*: r. mwut, pr. o-mwun-mwut-an, -ei, p.p. ko-mwun-mwut-an.
  *To chop to a point*: r. let, pr. o-let-an, -ei, pp. ka-let-an.

  *A circumcised youth*, torus-ion, pl. torus-tin.
  *A circumcised girl*, chemer-ion, pl. chemer-e-tin.
  *Circumcision ceremony*, tūm, pl. tum-w-ei; tum-tin.
- **Clan** (*family*), ôr, pl. ôr-ten (*vide* **Road**); pereris, pl. pereris-tin.

- **Claw, to** (*v.*): r. kôl, pr. o-kūl-an, -ei, p.p. kô-kôl-an.

- **Clean, to**: r. mwăt, pr. o-mwet-an, -ei, p.p. ka-mwat-an.

  *To clear ground for planting*: r. tĕr, pr. o-ter-an, -ei, p.p. ko-ter-an.

  *To close eyes*, r. nuch, pr. o-nuch-an, ei, p.p. ko-nuch-an.
  *To close fist*, r. maman, pr. o-memen-an, -ei, p.p. ka-maman-an.
- **Close** (*adv.*), legit.
- **Cloth**, anang-a, pl. anang-is (pl. = *'clothes'*).
- **Clothe, to**: o-ret-ei'-nyende ananga, *I will clothe him*.
- **Cloud**, pult-oʻ, pl. pult-oi.
- **Club, a**, rungū, pl. rungū-s.
  *Club-handle*, ket-ô-rungu.
  *Club-head*, met-o-rungu.
- **Coax, to**: r. mwagh; o-mwagh-an nyende môt, *I will coax him*.
- **Cock**, *nil*; *use* kiripo-kuku (Sw.).
  *Cock-bird*, kiripo-tarit.
  *Comb of cock guinea-fowl*, kuyagh, pl. kūi.
  *Spur*, sĕgh, pl. si'-w-oi. *Vide* **Claw**, *id.*
- **Cockroach**, selep-chon, pl. selep.
- **Coition, to have**: r. ruw, *sleep*; 'o-ruw-an, -ei, ngô kôrkôʻ.'
  *Illegally*: r. kum, pr. o-kum-an, -ei, p.p. ko-kum-an.
- **Cold** (*adj.*), kaghet, pl. kaghet-ei.
  *A cold in head*, tŭñg; o-m-a' tūñg-ô, *I have a cold*.
- **Collar bone**, kôwô-kat, pl. kô-ya-kat.
- **Collect, to**: r. ghamit, pr. o-ghomit-oi-an, p.p. ka-ghamit-an.
- **Colour**, munyan. *Vide* **Mud** *for head-dress*, *id.*
  *Black and dark blue*, togh, pl. tu-w-ech.
  *Black and white striped*, nya-le-chemarmăr, pl. cha-le-chemormŏr, *lit. zebra-like*.
  *Piebald*, nya-le-meril, pl. cha-le-meril-tin; meril = a many-coloured thing: a leopard.
  *Light blue and green*, nyaril, pl. nyaril-ech.
  *Brown*, tolelio, pl. tolelos-eni.
  *Kharki and yellow*, mirion-do, pl. mirion-ech.
  *Brown and white*, nya-l'-akori, pl. cha l'akori-tin (*giraffe like*).
- **Come, to**: r. mwun, pr. o-mwun-ei, p.p. ka-mwun-an *irreg.*
  *To arrive*: r. tit, pr. o-tit-oi-an, p.p. ka-tit-an.
  *To come apart*: r. ñgat, pr. o-ñgat-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgat-an.
  *To come behind*: r. lĕt (*adv.*), o-let-un-an, p.p. ko-let-u-an; *or* o-mwun-a' let-u, *I will come behind*.
  *To come near*: r. legĭt (*adv.*), o-legīt-itun-an-nyende *I am coming near him*.
  *To come from*: by position. Ki-aumwun-a' Kerut, *I came from Weiwei*.
  *To come in*: r. tu, pr. o-tu-n-an, p.p. ko-tu-an.
  *To come for*: ka-mwun-an a-met-inyi ôr *I have come to escort you on the road*.
  *To come round*: r. wet, pr. o-wit-an, -ei, p.p. ka-wēt-an.

- **Come, to** (*cont.*):
  *I come upon a man*: r. nyô, pr. o-nyô-soi-an ngô chi, p.p. ka-nyôr-i-an.
  *To come with any one*: o-mwun-ei, tokol ngô chi, *I come with a man*.
- **Command, to**: r. ruwok,¹ pr. o-ruwok-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ruwok-an.

- **Conduct, to**: r. p. *Vide* **Carry** *under* **Bear**.

- **Cook, to**: r. ghŏgh, pr. o-ghogh-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ghogh-an.

- **Cunning**, ñgohom, pl. ñgohom-ech.
- **Cup, a** (*half calabash*), kurit, pl. kurit-ai.
  v. (*vide* **To bleed**): r. char, pr. o-cher-an, p.p. ka-char-an.
  *One who cups*, char-in, pl. char-u.
  *Cupping-horn*, kulo-won, pl. kulō.
- **Cure, to**: r. sop, pr. o-sop-it-an, p.p. kô-sop-it-an.
- **Current** (*of a river*), mughulō. *Vide* **Heart**, *id.*
- **Curse, to**: r. mwagh. *Vide* **Abuse**.
- **Cut, to**: r. mwŭt, pr. o-mwut-an, -ei, p.p. ka-mwut-an.
  *To slash*: r. ter, pr. o-tĕr-an, -ei, p.p. kô-ter-an.
  *To cut in pieces*: r. mwut, pr. o-mwun-mwut-an, p.p. ko-mwun-mwut-an.
  *To cut to a point*: r. let, pr. o-let-an, p.p. ka-let-an.

### D

- **Daily**, ñgat-ñgat.
- **Damage, to**: r. nyar, pr. o-nyor-an, p.p. ka-nyar-an.
- **Dance, to**: r. tiarañgmw, pr. o-tiarañgmw-an, -ei, p.p. ka-tiarañgmw-an; (ii) r. doñguny, pr. o-doñguny-an, -ei, p.p. ka-doñguny-an.

- **Dare, to**: r. rŏs. *Vide* **Try**.
- **Dark**, togh, pl. tu-w-ech. *Vide* **Night**, *id.*
- **Daub, to** (*a house with mud*): r. mal, pr. o-môl-an, -ei, p.p. ka-māl-an.
  (*oil on the body*): r. iyil, pr. o-iyil-an, -ei, p.p. ka-iyil-an.
  (*mud on the hair*): r. tich, pr. o-tich-an, p.p. kô-tich-an.
- **Daughter**, chep-toʻ, pl. tī-pin.
- **Dawn** (*early*), kôrūn.
- **Day**, asis, pl. ōsis.
  *Another day*, asis-t-anga.

  ko-koriol-anan, *I am dazzled*.

- **Death**, meghat.

- **Deter, to**: r. ghiet, pr. o-ghiet-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ghiet-an; (ii) r. mir.

- **Dewlap** (*of oxen*), thaghaʻ, pl. theghiʻ.
  (*of sheep*), kalablap, pl. kalablap-ai.

- **Disappear, to**: r. lit, pr. o-lit-an, -ei, p.p. ko-lit-an (of moon in clouds, &c.).

  *To lose*: r. mat, o-mat-oi-an, p.p. ka-mat-an; (ii) pot-it.

- **Distribute, to**: r. pār, pr. o-pôr-an, p.p. ka-pār-an.

  *What's to be done? (What will you do?)*, i-luguh-ne?
  *Food is cooked*; or *Work is done*, ka-ko-wany-oʻ!
  *I'm doing nothing*, m'o-gh-an-nye kue!

  *Wicker door*, terip-a, pl. terip-ai.

- **Dream, to**: r. ruw, *sleep*, pr. o-ruwatat-oi-an, p.p. ko-ruwatat-an.
- **Dress, to** (*intrans. and trans.*): r. lach, pr. o-lech-an, -ei, p.p. ka-lach-an.
- **Dress**, anangis (pl. of anang-a, *cloth*).
  *Of skins*, ser-a, pl. ser-ô.
- **Drink, to** (*of milk*): r. lu, pr. o-lu-an, -ei, p.p. ko-lu-an.
  (*of water*): r. igh, pr. o-igh-an, -ei, p.p. ka-igh-an.
  *Drinking-place*, ōghen, pl. ōghen-tis.
- **Drip, to**: r. chunu, pr. 3rd s. chunu, p.p. 3rd s. ka-chunu.

  *To drive away*: r. on, pr. o-on-an, p.p. ka-on-an.

  (*trans.*): r. wirt (*vide* **Throw away**, *id.*); (ii) r. tiart (*vide under* **Fall**).
- **Drown, to**: r. chut, pr. o-chut-an, -ei, p.p. ko-chut-an.

- **Drum**: nil.

  *A drunken person*, tamas-in, pl. tamas-ŭ, -i.

  *Drying place for grain*, rukor, pl. rukor-ai.
- **Dumb**, mingāt-e, pl. mingôt-enei. *Vide* **Deaf**.

- **Each**, tukul, tokol.

  *Wood placed in hole at top of ear*, ket-pa-yit.

  *Sand*, ñgaiyam (coll.).

  *I eat with a man*, o-m-an ngô chi.

- **Eighty**, konom-ngô-sosom (50 + 30).

- **Fable, a**, kikôren, pl. *id.*
- **Face**, tokach, pl. tokuch.
- **Faint, to**: r. tomeghigh,¹ pr. o-tomeghigh-an, ei, p.p. ka-tomeghigh-an.
  *To feel faint from illness*: r. iyīn, pr. o-iyīn-an, p.p. ka-iyīn-an.
- **Fall, to**: r. pĕt, pr. o-pet-an, -ei, p.p. ka-pet-an.
  *To fall into*: r. pĕt, pr. o-pet-in-an, -ei, p.p. kō-pet-i-an.
  *To make to fall*: r. tiart, pr. o-tiort-oi-an, p.p. ka-tiart-an.
  *To fall from above*: r. chint, pr. o-chint-oi-an, p.p. ka-chint-an.
  *To fall like rain*: r. chun, pr. o-chun-un-an, p.p. ka-chun-u-an.
  *To fall (of a tree)*: r. rūl, pr. o-rūl-an, -ei, p.p. ko-rul-an.
  *To fell a tree*: r. tĕr, pr. o-ter-an, -ei, p.p. kô-tĕr-an.
  *To fall sick*: r. miân, pr. o-miôn-an, p.p. ka-miân-an.
- **Falsehoods, one who speaks**, put-in, pl. put-ŭ.
- **Family**, ôr, pl. ôr-ten.
  *Father of bride*, kapikoi, pl. kapikoi-tin.
  *id., if he is a chief*, ŏpoi, pl. ŏpoi-tin.
  *Wives of bride's father, including actual mother*, pô-ker, pl. pô-ker-tin.
  *Mother-in-law*, po-mwa, pl. pa-mwô-tin. *Vide under* **Mother-in-law**.
- **Famine**, kamei, pl. kamei-tin.

> ¹ Evidently same root as to die, *megh*.

- **Fan, a**, pkonya, pl. pkonya-tin.

- **Fast** (*adv.*), lawel; pi'-cha-le-lawel, *fast runners*; (ii) pertat, pl. pertit-ei.
- **Fasten, to**: r. rat. *Vide* **Bind**.
- **Fat**, mwagh, pl. mwai'-tin; (ii) mwowon (coll.).
  (*adj.*) terus, pl. terus-ech.
  *to become fat*: r. terus, pr. o-terus-itun-an, p.p. ka-terus-itu-an; ocha terus, *I am fat*.
- **Father** (*offspring speaking*), bapu, pl. bapu-tin.
  (*any one else speaking*), kwanda, pl. kwanda-tin.

  *Father-in-law (wife speaking)*, pô-tich, pl. pô-ti'-tin.

- **Fault, a**, ke-tos-en, pl. *id.*
- **Fear, to**: r. wagh, pr. o-wegh-an, p.p. ka-wagh-an.
- **Feast-day**, tum, pl. tum-tin. *Vide* **Dance**.
- **Feather, a**, karor-ion, pl. karor.
  *Feathers of arrows*, karor (pl. *used collectively*).
  *Ostrich feathers*, songol, pl. *id.*
- **Feeble person, a**, kersañg, pl. kersoñg.
- **Feed cattle, to**: r. iyakwiy, pr. o-iyokwiy-an, -ei, p.p. ka-iyakwiy-an.
  *To feed (intr.)*: r. kĕt, pr. o-kĕt-ei, p.p. ka-kĕt-an.

  (*with hands*): r. tătăp, o-tetep-ei, p.p. ka-tatap-an.
  *To grope in dark*: r. sam, pr. o-semsem-an, p.p. ka-samsam-an.
- **Fell, to** (*of trees*): r. tĕr. *Vide under* **Fall**.

  (*animal*), teta, pl. topa, toka. *Vide* **Gender**, p. 50.
  *Female (adj.)*, sakāte, pl. sakôti.
  *One that has conceived*, īwok, pl. īwok-ai.
  *One that has not yet conceived*, marar, pl. marar-en.
  *Uncircumcised*, sōrin, pl. sôr.
  *Barren person or animal*, sunô, pl. sunō-n, (*adj.*) nya-le-sunô.
- **Fence, a**, kasar, pl. kasir.
  *To fence*: r. woch, pr. o-woch-an, -ei, p.p. ka-woch-an.

- **Few**, cho-ñgeri.
- **Field**, bar, pl. bar-en.
  (*fallow*), rôr-ô, pl. rôr-on.
- **Fierce**, korōm, pl. korōm-ech.
- **Fifteen**, taman-ngô-mūt (10 + 5).
- **Fifth**, nya-pa-mūt, nya-le-mūt.
- **Fifty**, konom.
- **Fight with, to**: r. pôr, pr. o-pôr-soi-an, p.p. kô-pôr-i-an; o-mwô-chi-n-a' Lorimo ka-por-io ngô Turkana, *I will cause Lorimo to fight the Turkana*.
  *A fight*, luk, pl. luk-us; (ii) poriot, pl. poriot-in.
- **Fill, to**: r. nyegh, pr. o-nyegh-itan, -ei, p.p. ka-nyeghit-an.

  *To fill a hole*: r. tip, pr. a-tip-an, -ei, p.p. ko-tip-an.
  *To fill with food, to be full*: r. pughoñgy, pr. o-pughoñgy-an, ei, p.p. ko-pughoñgy-an.

- **Filth**, sim-nyon, pl. sim.

- **Fool, a**, selw-aʻ, pl. selw-ei.

  *To follow*, r. rĕp, pr. o-rīp-an, -ei, p.p. ko-rep-an.
  *I go alone*, o-wet'-ungit.
  *To go away from*: r. pakach, pr. o-pokoch-an, -ei, p.p. ka-pakach-an.
  *To go away in morning*: r. wast, pr. o-wast-oi-an, p.p. ka-wast-an.
  *To go back*: r. mĕl, pr. o-mel-oi-an, p.p. ka-mel-an.
  *I went behind*, ko-w-an let-u.
  *To go bad*: r. nun, pr. o-nun-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nun-an.
  *To go first*: r. nyaiy, pr. o-nyoiy-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nyaiy-an.
  *To go behind (follow)*: r. rĕp, pr. o-rip-an, -ei, p.p. ko-rep-an.
  *To go before*: r. tôwôgh, pr. o-tōwōgh-an, -ei, p.p. kô-tôwôgh-an.
  *To go down*: r. rēt, pr. o-rēt-oi-an, p.p. ko-rēt-an.
  *To go for a walk*: r. weset, pr. o-weset-oi-an, p.p. ko-weset-an.
  *I went in his stead*: ki-ow-an wal-ô-mi-nye.
  *To go into*: r. lit, pr. o-lit-an, -ei, p.p. ko-lit-an; (ii) r. t.
  *To go lame*: r. ñgwal, pr. o-ñgwôl-an, p.p. ka-ñgwal-an.
  *To go near*: r. legit, pr. o-legit-itu-n-an, p.p. ka-legit-itu-an.
  *To go out*: r. ñget, pr. o-ñget-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñget-an.
  *To go out (of fire)*: [*Vide* **Die**] ka-maʻ maʻ, *The fire has gone out*.
  *To go over*: r. ŏgh, pr. ōgh-soi-an, p.p. ko-ogh-i-an.
  *To go past*: r. wēr, pr. o-wēr-an, -ei, p.p. ka-wēr-an.
  *To go round*: r. witüit, o-wītüit-an, -ei, ka-witüit-an.
  *To go running*: r. rīp, pr. o-rip-an, p.p. ko-rip-an.
  *Go away!* wi-y-a! pl. a-pe! (imp.).
  *To go to battle*: r. set, pr. o-set-an, ei, p.p. ka-set-an.
  *To go up*: r. lany, pr. o-lony-an, p.p. ka-lany-an; (ii) r. pus, pr. o-pus-an, -ei, p.p. ka-pus-an.
  *I go up and down*, o-rēt-oi-an a-pus.

> ¹ There is no verb in Suk meaning 'to be healthy'. The Nandi *cham-ke*, however, is found in the phrases of salutation, e.g. *chamke-kau*, which apparently means 'health to your kraal!' *vide* p. 91. 'I am healthy', however, is rendered by *ocha satat-e*, or *otiñget-a' sapon*: *Chamke-sapon!* = good health to you!

  *I have hiccoughs*, ko-wir-ana' nigich (*lit. I am hit with . . .*).
- **Hide** (*of any animal*), min-yon, pl. min.
  (*of oxen*), añgw-a, pl. añgw-oi.
  (*of sheep or goats*), sera, pl. serô.

  (*intrans.*): r. ghup, pr. o-ghup-an, p.p. ka-ghup-an.
- **High**, kōgh, pl. kōgh-ech.
- **Hill**, kutuñg, pl. kutuñg-ai.
  *Gentle slope*, lakam, pl. lakam-is.
  *Summit*, rong-ô, pl. rong-on.
- **Hilt**, kunyŭk, pl. kunyūk.
- **Him**, nyende.
- **Hinder, to** (*by making a noise*): r. siel, pr. o-siel-jin-an, p.p. ko-siel-j-an; (ii) r. sĕr, pr. o-ser-oi-an, p.p. ka-ser-an.
- **Hip**, lep, pl. lep-is-ei.
- **Hippopotamus**, mokŏgh, pl. mokōgh; (ii) endaruka, pl. endaruka-tin.
- **His**, nyenyi, pl. chechi; -(i)nyi, -(e)chi, p. 70.
- **Hit, to** (*e.g. with a spear*): r. păr, pr. o-per-an, -ei, p.p. ka-par-an.
  (*e.g. with a stick*): r. muny, pr. o-muny-an, -ei, p.p. ka-muny-an.
- **Hither**, yi.
  *Hither and thither*, yi ngô tino.
- **Hitherto**, pa-kainy.
- **Hock**, toi-yon, pl. toi.
- **Hoe** (*wooden*), kapemb-oʻ, pl. kapemb-ei.
- **Hoist, to**: r. ten, pr. o-ten-an, -ei, p.p. kô-ten-an; (ii) r. sit, pr. o-sit-an, -ei, p.p. ko-sit-an.
- **Hold, to**: r. nam, pr. o-nem-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nam-an.
  (*in the open palm*): r. karkar, pr. o-korkor-an, -ei, p.p. ka-karkar-an.
  (*in closed fist*): r. mumit, pr. o-mumit-an, -ei, p.p. ka-mumit-an.
- **Hole**, prim, pl. prim-p-ot. *Vide* **Ditch**.
  (*through lower lip*), wotut, wertut, pl. wertut-ai.

  *Hollow thing, a*, pololtei, pl. pololtei-tin.
- **Home** (*man speaking*), kata-nyan.
  *Home-stayer, a*, tep-ô-kau, pl. tep-is-ô-kau.
- **Honey**, komat (coll.).
  *Honey-barrel*, mweghin, pl. mweghin-tis.
  *Hive*, ponat.
  *Honeycomb*, yiti'-ponat (*lit. ears of the hive*).
  *Honey wine*, koman.
- **Honour** (*s.*), konyit.
  *To honour*: r. kanyit, pr. o-konyit-an, -ei, p.p. ka-kanyit-an.
- **Hoof**, putul, pl. putul-ai.
- **Hope, to.** *Vide* **Want**, *id.*
- **Horn**, kūyagh, pl. kūĭ.
  *Drinking-horn*, soghoʻ.
- **Hornet** (*mason bee?*), korom-in, pl. karam.
- **Horse**, añgoli, pl. añgoli-tin.
- **Hot**; *it is hot*, mi-maʻ.
  *I am hot.* *Vide sup. under* **Heat**.
- **House**, kô, kō, kŏt, pl. kôr, kôr-in.
  *Milkshelf*, asach-a, pl. asach-is.
  *Outside space for warriors to sleep*, aperit, pl. aperit-is.
  *Cave*, kepen, pl. kepen-is, kepen-ot.
  *I stay in a house*, o-tep-oi-a' kô.
- **How?** lata-ne? le-ne.
  *How are you?* ichi karam?
  *How often?* ar'-ata?
  *How many?* ata?
- **However**, walô.
- **Human**, pa-chi.
- **Hump**, wuk, pl. wuk-w-ei.
- **Hump-backed**, tokom, pl. tukum-ech.
- **Hundred**, pokol, pl. pokol-oi.
- **Hunger**, kamei, pl. *id.* or kamei-tin.
  *I am hungry*, kam-ana' kamei.
- **Hunt, to**: r. marar,¹ pr. o-merer-an, -ei, p.p. ka-marar-an.
- **Hunter**, merer-in, pl. merer-ŭ.
- **Hurry** (*s.*), koskos.
  (*v.*): r. kaskas, pr. o-koskos-an. *Vide* **Haste**.
- **Hurt, to**: r. sĕr, pr. o-ser-an, -ei, p.p. ka-sēr-an.
- **Husband**, chi-tañgu, pl. pi-koku.
- **Husk**, mŭr.
- **Hut**, kô, pl. kor. *Vide* **House**.
  *In a cornfield*, kô-pa-pagh.
  *For circumcision*, menj-oʻ, pl. menj-ei.
  *Warriors' tree where meat is eaten*, kerket, pl. kerket-is.
- **Hyaena**, kawagh, pl. kowŏgh.

> ¹ *Vide* **Chase**, r. mar, of which this is the reduplicated form.

### I

- **I**, ani; *suffix* -an; *prefix* o-, &c., *vide under verbs*.
- **Idle person, an**, kērsang, pl. kērsong.
- **If**, *prefix* atô, *vide* p. 79. *Also* anda, *with*, p. 80, note 1.
- **Ignorant person, an**, sel-waʻ, pl. selw-ei.
- **Ill, to be**: r. miān, pr. o-miôn-an, -ei, p.p. ka-miān-an.
  *I am very ill*, ocha kasartei.
- **Ill person, an**, miôn-ei, pl. miôn-sut.
- **Illnesses**:
  *Abscess*, tasôs, pl. tesūs.
  *Boils or pimples*, kwech (coll.).
  *Catarrh or cold*, tuñg.
  *Cough*, karal.
  *Dropsy*, paras.
  *Liver disease*, tērmes.
  *Spleen disease*, ñgasat.
  *Malaria*, yamat. *Vide* **Wind**, *id.*
  *Sore throat*, komoghok.

- **Immature**, meneñg, pl. mingech.
- **Immediately**, yĕ.
- **Implore, to**: r. som, pr. o-som-an, -ei, p.p. ka-som-an.

  *In order that.* [Use subj.]
  *In place of*, wal-ô-mi.
  *In the middle of*, kwin.
  *In the morning*, tokwagha.
  *In the evening*, menūn.
- **Increase, to**: r. tas, pr. o-tes-an, -ei, p.p. ka-tas-an.
- **Indeed**, man; nya-man, kochiyu.

- **Information**, lugoi; (ii) ñgolion.
- **Inhabit, to**: r. tĕp, pr. o-tep-oi-an, p.p. kô-tep-an.
- **Insect**, ngôt-ian, pl. ngôt.
- **Inside**, ōrī; mi ori, *he is inside*.
- **Instantly**, yĕ.
- **Insult, to**: r. mwagh, pr. o-mwogh-an, -ei, p.p. ka-mwagh-an.
- **Inter, to**: r. tĭp, pr. o-tip-an, -ei, p.p. kō-tip-an.
- **Intercede, Intercept, to**: r. ghet, pr. o-ghet-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ghet-an.
- **Interrupt, to**: r. nyor, pr. o-nyor-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nyor-an.
- **Intestines**, kwan-yan, pl. kwan, kwan-tin.
- **Intoxicated, to be**: r. tamas, pr. o-temes-an, -ei, p.p. ka-tamas-an.
- **Investigate**: r. rŏs, pr. o-rŏs-an, -ei, p.p. kô-rŏs-an.
- **Iron**, karĕn, pl. karin.
  *Iron ore*, ñgoriyim.
  *Refuse of above*, piya'-karen.
- **Irritate, to**: r. ghom, pr. o-ghom-soi-an, p.p. kō-ghom-an; (ii) r. rum.
- **Issue from, to**: r. ñget, pr. o-ñget-an, p.p. ka-ñget-an.
- **It**, nyende.
- **Itch**, kwech.
  *I have an itching*, o-ñgwer-a' kwech.
- **Its**, nyenyi, &c. *Vide* **His**, **Hers**, *id.*
- **Ivory**, kēlăt, pl. kelăt.

- **Lean against, to**: r. tīñg, pr. o-tiñg-an, p.p. kō-tiñg-an; (ii) r. chichim; o-chichim-an ô legup, *I lean on a stick*.
- **Leap, to**: r. doñguny.

- **Leather** (*ox*), min-yon, pl. mĭn.
  (*sheep and goat*), sera, pl. serô.
- **Leave, to** (*go away*): r. wăt, pr. o-wet-an, -ei, ka-wat-an; (ii) r. w, pr. o-w-an, -ei, p.p. ko-w-an.
  (*come away*), r. ñget, pr. o-ñget-an, p.p. ka-ñget-an.
  *To leave alone*: r. pist, pr. o-pist-oi-an, p.p. kō-pist-an.
  *To throw away*: r. wirt, pr. o-wirt-oi-an, p.p. ka-wirt-an.
  *To let go*: r. tiagh, pr. o-tiogh-an, p.p. ka-tiagh-an. *Vide* **Untie**.

### M

- **Mad person, a**, kipoiyi, pl. kipoiyi-tin.
- **Maggot, a**, ngôt-ian, pl. ngôt.
  *Another kind which enters the stalks of crops*, kasow-a, pl. kasow-ai.
- **Magic, to practise**: r. pan, pr. o-pen-an, -ei, p.p. ka-pan-an.
- **Magician**, pōn-in, pl. pon-ŭ.
- **Maize**, pembe (coll.).
  *Stalk*, mochen, pl. mōpa.
- **Make, to**: r. gh, pr. o-gh-an, -ei, p.p. ka-gh-an; 3rd sing. pr. igh-ei; 3rd sing. p.p. ke-gh.
  *To make equal or like*, o-gh-a' ki-le-nyô-nyi.
  *To make haste*: r. kos(kos). *Vide* **Haste**.
  *To make iron articles*: r. tañg, pr. o-toñg-an, -ei, p.p. ka-tañg-an.
  *To make water*: r. sokus, pr. o-sukos-an, -ei, p.p. kô-sukus-an.
  *To make well.* *Vide* **To cure**.

  *Bull*, kiruk, pl. kīr.

- **Polish, to** (*by rubbing*): r. sut, pr. o-sut-an, -ei, p.p. kō-sut-an.
  (*by scraping with a knife*): r. ñgôñgôr, pr. o-ñgôñgôr-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgôñgôr-an.
- **Pond** (*water-hole*), takar, pl. takar-ai.
- **Ponder, to**: r. nonsi, pr. o-nonsi-an, p.p. ko-nonsi-an.
- **Poor person**, mi-won, pl. mi.
  (*destitute*), panan, pl. penon.
- **Porcupine**, sabĕt, pl. sabīt.
  (*quills*), kata sabet (lit. *thorns*).
- **Porridge**, musar.
  *I cook porridge*, o-chil-a' musar.
- **Porter** (*slave*), metuar-in, pl. metuar.
- **Portion**, karēran, pl. *id.*
- **Possessions**, tukun (pl. of ki, *a thing*).
- **Possibly**, teptoi.
- **Pot**, tĕr, pl. ter-in.
- **Potsherd**, kerpas-iaʻ, pl. kerpis.
- **Potter**, koko-tĕr (*lit. the old woman of the pot*).
- **Potter's clay**, mĕno (coll.).

- **Pound, to**: r. nyagh, pr. o-nyogh-an, p.p. ka-nyagh-an.
- **Pour, to**: r. iyin, pr. o-iyin-an, p.p. ka-iyin-an.
  *To pour away*: r. iyit, pr. o-iyit-oi-an, p.p. ka-iyit-an.
- **Powder**, pĕsia (coll.).
  *Gun*, kitiōlu (*i.e. an explosive sound*), pl. kitiol-in.
- **Powerful**, añgēr, pl. oñger-ech.
- **Pray, to**: r. som, pr. o-som-an, -ei, p.p. kô-som-an.
- **Prayer**, som-at-in.
- **Precede, to**: r. tôwôgh, pr. o-tōwōgh-an, p.p. kô-tôwôgh-an.
- **Prefer, to**: r. cham. *Vide* **Love**, &c., *id.*
- **Pregnant, to be**: r. mīr, pr. o-mir-an, -ei, p.p. ko-mir-an.
  *Pregnant*, mīr-ei.
- **Prepare, to**: r. gh. *Vide* **Make**, *id.*
- **Present, to**: r. ten, pr. o-ten-an, p.p. kô-ten-an.
- **Presently**, tôma-nye kio-meneng.
- **Press, to**: r. pir, pr. o-pir-an, p.p. kō-pir-an.
- **Prevent, to**: r. mīr, pr. o-mir-an, p.p. ko-mir-an; komira' nyende mowoʻ, *I prevented him from going*.
  *To refuse*: r. tagh, pr. o-tegh-an, -ei, p.p. ka-tagh-an.
- **Prick, to**: r. rit, pr. o-rit-an, -ei, p.p. ko-rit-an.
- **Prisoner of war**, pero-won, pl. pero.
- **Privy** (*secret*), chom-ei (?).
  *I go to the privy*, o-wet-a' piyat.
- **Proceed, to.** *Vide* **Go**, *id.*
- **Procure, to**: r. sich, pr. o-sich-an, p.p. ko-sich-an.
  *I will procure a cow for you*, o-sik-oi-a' tainy nyeñgmwunyi.
- **Prod, to**: r. parpar, pr. o-perper-an, -ei, p.p. ka-parpar-an.
- **Prohibit, to**: r. tagh. *Vide* **Forbid**.
- **Prop up, to**: r. soch, pr. o-soch-an, -ei, p.p. ko-soch-an.
- **Properly**, nya-man.
- **Property**, tukun. *Vide* **Possessions**.
- **Prophecy, to**: r. ñgôr, pr. o-ñgôr-an, -ei, p.p. ko-ñgôr-an.
- **Prostitute**, che-pores, pl. che-pores-ai (lit. *a person of no account*); pures, *goat's dung*.
- **Puff, to**: r. s, pr. o-s-an, -ei, p.p. ka-s-an.
  *Puffed-up person*, nisan-ei, nison-sut.
- **Pull, to**: r. ñgus, pr. o-ñgus-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgus-an.
  *To pull out*: r. chit, pr. o-chit-an, -ei, p.p. ko-chit-an.
  *To pull out a tooth*: r. ñgus, pr. o-ñgus-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgus-an.
- **Pumpkin**, menyen, pl. menyen-tin.
- **Pure**, tilil, pl. tilil-ech.
- **Purgative**, mugatan, pl. mugatan-tin.
- **Purge, to**: r. iyīt, pr. o-iyit-oi-an, p.p. ka-iyit-an.
- **Purpose**, ñgol-ion, pl. ñgal.
  *To do on purpose*: r. kwet, pr. o-kwet-in-an, p.p. ko-kwet-i-an.
- **Pursue, to**: r. rep, pr. o-rip-an, -ei, p.p. ko-rep-an.
  *To hunt*: r. marar, pr. o-merer-an, -ei, p.p. ka-marar-an.
  *To seek*: r. iyar, pr. o-iyer-an, -ei, p.p. ka-iyar-an.
- **Pus**, perut-it.
- **Push, to**: r. yumt, pr. o-yumt-oi-an, p.p. ko-yumt-an.
- **Put, to**: r. tĕn, pr. o-ten-an, -ei, p.p. kô-ten-an.
  *To put in the way*: r. kut, pr. o-kut-an, -ei, p.p. ka-kut-an.
  *To put down*: r. ñgūs, pr. o-ñgus-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgūs-an.

*(Page 134)*

- **Put, to** (*cont.*):
  *To put on clothes*: r. lach, pr. o-loch-an, -ei, p.p. ka-lach-an.
  *To put out fire*: r. mĭs, pr. o-mis-an, -ei, p.p. kō-mis-an.
  *To put out fire with water*: r. nūch, pr. o-nuch-an, -ei, p.p. kô-nuch-an.
  *To put to (shut)*: r. kar, pr. o-ker-an, p.p. ka-kar-an.
  *To put to flight*: r. on, pr. o-on-an, p.p. ka-on-an.
  *I will put to rights*, o-gh-a' karam-et.
  *I put wood (on a fire)*, o-iyin-a' kwen.

### Q

- **Quake, to**: r. potan, pr. o-poten-an, p.p. kô-potan-an.
- **Quarrel, to**: r. sial, pr. o-sial-an, -ei, p.p. ka-sial-an.
  *A quarrel (subs.)*, sial-aʻ, pl. sial-in. *Vide* **Noise**, *id.*
  *A quarrelsome person*, sial-in, pl. sial-ŭ.
  *To settle a quarrel*: r. ghiet, pr. o-ghiet-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ghiet-an.
- **Quench, to** (*a fire*): r. nūch, pr. o-nuch-an, -ei, p.p. kô-nuch-an.
- **Question, to**: r. tērp, pr. o-tērp-an, p.p. ka-tērp-an.
- **Quick**, (i) láwél, pl. lawél; (ii) pertat, pl. pertit-ei.
- **Quiet**, (i) telene, pl. tolosi; (ii) sisat, pl. sisot.
  *To be quiet*: r. sis, pr. o-sis-an, -ei, p.p. ka-sis-an.
- **Quietly**, môt.
- **Quit, to**: r. ñget, pr. o-ñget-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñget-an.
- **Quite**, man, nya-man.
- **Quiver, to**: r. potan. *Vide* **Quake**.
  *A quiver*, merongĭt, pl. merongīt.

### R

- **Rabbit**, blĕrkw-a, pl. blĕrkw-oi. *Vide* **Hare**.
- **Race a person, to**: r. pel, *overcome*, pr. o-pel-jin-soi-an (pertitioʻ), p.p. ko-pel-j-an.
- **Rafter**, mero-won, pl. mero.
- **Rag**, oko-won, pl. oko.
- **Raid, to**: r. set, pr. o-sĕt-an, -ei, p.p. ka-sĕt-an.
  *A raider*, sĕt-in, pl. set-ŭ.
- **Rain**, ilat.
  *To rain*: r. robon, pr. o-robon-an, p.p. ka-robon-an; ka-robon ilat omatu, *yesterday it rained*.
  *It rains*, robon-ei.
- **Rainbow**, kuntopol, pl. kuntopol-ai.
- **Raise, to**: r. sit, pr. o-it-an, -ei, p.p. ko-sit-an.
- **Ransom, to**: r. tiach, pr. o-tioch-an, -ei, p.p. ka-tiach-an.
- **Rap, to**: r. koiñgkoiny, pr. o-koiñgkoiny-an, p.p. kô-koiñgkoiny-an.
- **Rash, to come out in a**: r. siew, pr. o-siew-un-an, p.p. ko-sew-u-an.
- **Rat** (*of all kinds, and mice*), mer-ian, pl. mer.
- **Rather**, mwañgwa.
- **Rations**, om-isioʻ, pl. omisio-yut-(ūt).

- **Razor**, rotwa-met.
- **Reach, to**: r. tit, pr. o-tit-oi-an, ka-tit-an.
  (*to amount to*): r. t.
- **Ready**, a-ny'-eti, pl. a-ch'-eti.
  *I am ready*, a-ny'-eti ani.
  *It is not yet ready*, tôma-ny' a-ny-eti, pl. tôma-ch'ach-eti.
  *To be ready*, r. rir, pr. o-rir-an, p.p. ka-rir-an.
- **Reap, to**: r. kas, pr. o-kes-an, -ei, p.p. ka-kas-an.
- **Rear, to**: r. rŏs, pr. o-ros-an, p.p. kô-ros-an.

- **Receive, to** (*cont.*):
  *To take*: r. nam, (ii) r. cheñg. *Vide* **Take**.
  *To accept*: r. cham. *Vide* **Like**, *id.*
  *I will take for him*, o-nem-chin-an (nyende).
- **Reckon, to**: r. iyĭt, pr. o-iyit-an, -ei, p.p. ko-iyit-an.
- **Recline, to**: r. 'ruw'. *Vide* **Sleep**, *id.*
  *To rest (breathe)*: r. muny, pr. o-muny-an, -ei, p.p. ko-muny-an.
  *To rest*: r. nuch, pr. o-nuch-an, -ei, p.p. ko-nuch-an.
- **Recognize, to**: r. nget, pr. o-nget-an, -ei, p.p. kô-nget-an.
- **Recollect, to**: r. nān, pr. o-non-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nan-an.
- **Rectum**, masow-a, pl. masow-ei.
- **Red**, pirir, pl. pirech.
  *To become red*: r. pirir, pr. o-pirir-an, -ei, p.p. ko-pirir-an.
- **Redeem, to**: r. tiach. *Vide* **ransom**.
- **Reduce, to**: r. ñger, pr. o-ñger-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñger-an.
- **Reed, a**, moikŭt, pl. moikūt.
  *Bullrush*, kamatigh-an, pl. kamatigh.
- **Reflect, to**: from r. non (*recollect*), r. nonsi, pr. o-nonsi-an, kô-nonsi-an.
  *To reflect an image*: r. rial, pr. o-riol-an, -ei, p.p. ka-rial-an.
- **Refuse, to**: r. tagh. *Vide* **Prohibit**.
- **Refute, to**: r. pēl, pr. o-pel-an, p.p. kô-pēl-an.
- **Reject, to**: r. tagh. *Vide* **Prohibit**, *imp.*
- **Rejoice, to**: r. ñgarech, pr. o-ñgarech-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgarech-an.
- **Relate, to**: r. kôr, pr. o-kôr-an, -ei, p.p. kô-kôr-an.
- **Relative**, wērinja, pl. wērkecha.
- **Remain, to**: r. tep, pr. o-tep-oi-an, p.p. kô-tep-an.
  *To remain over*: r. ñgĕt, pr. o-ñget-un-an, p.p. ko-ñget-u-an.
  *Remainder*, cha-ñgetu.
- **Remember, to**: r. nān, pr. o-non-an, p.p. ka-nan-an.
- **Remind, to**: r. nān, pr. o-non-chin-an, p.p. kô-non-ch-an.
- **Remove**: r. cheñg. *Vide* **Take**.
  (ii) r. natagh, pr. o-notogh-oi-an, p.p. ka-natagh-an.
  *Remove it!* natagh-ena!
- **Rend, to**: r. karer, pr. o-kerer-an, -ei, p.p. ka-karer-an.
- **Repair, to**: r. nĕp. *Vide* **Mend**.
- **Repay, to**: r. ghait, pr. o-ghoit-oi-an, p.p. ka-ghait-an.
  *To repay a person*: r. ôk, pr. o-ôk-in-an, p.p. ko-ôk-i-an.
- **Reply, to**: (i) r. siropon, pr. o-siropon-un-an, p.p. ko-siropon-u-an; (ii) r. siropant. *Vide under* **Answer**.
- **Request, to** (*to wish*): r. mech, pr. o-mech-an, -ei, p.p. ka-mech-an.
  (*to pray*): r. som, pr. o-som-an, -ei, p.p. ka-som-an.
- **Rest, to** (*breathe*): r. muny, pr. o-muny-an, -ei, p.p. ko-muny-an.
  *To rest*: r. nuch. *Vide* **Recline**.
  *The rest*, w-alak.
- **Retire, to**: r. mĕl, pr. o-mel-oi-an, -ei, p.p. ka-mel-an.
- **Return, to** (*intr.*): (i) r. wark, pr. o-werk-oi-an, p.p. ka-wark-an; (ii) r. mel, pr. o-mel-oi-an, p.p. ka-mel-an.

- **Rib**, karas-ian, pl. karos.
- **Riches**, tukun.
- **Rich man**, mongor-ion, pl. mongôr.
- **Riddle**, tiangoi, pl. tiangoi-tin.

- **Ride on, to**: r. lany, pr. o-lony-an, -ei, p.p. ka-lany-an.
- **Ridicule, to**: r. rôr, pr. o-rôr-chin-an. *Vide* **Laugh at**.
- **Right** (*hand*), (ewu)-tagh.
- **Ring**, kai-moron,¹ pl. kai-morin, kai-moris-ai.
  *Ear-ring (wire)*, tai, pl. tauwoi.
  *(chain)*, sirim-nyon, pl. sirim-tin.
  *(wooden)*, ket-pa-yīt.
- **Ring** (*a bell*), **to**: r. sasach, pr. o-sosoch-an, -ei, p.p. ka-sasach-an.
- **Rip, to**: r. wun, pr. o-wūn-an, -ei, p.p. ko-wūn-an.
- **Ripe, to be**: r. rur, pr. o-rur-an, -ei, p.p. kô-rur-an.
  *Ripe*, kakur, pl. *id.*
- **Rise, to**: r. ñget, pr. o-ñget-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñget-an.
  *To stand up*: r. ghany, pr. o-ghony-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ghany-an.
- **River**, lalua, pl. lalua-ten.
  *Rivulet*, champel-ua, pl. champel-oi.
- **Road**, ôr, pl. ôr-ten.
- **Roar, to**: r. til, pr. o-til-an, -ei, p.p. ko-til-an.
- **Roast, to**: r. pal, pr. o-pel-an, -ei, p.p. ka-pal-an.
  *To roast in the skin*: r. lep, pr. o-lip-an, -ei, p.p. ka-lep-an.
  *Fat*: r. tol, pr. o-tol-an, -ei, p.p. kô-tol-an.
  *To make biltong*: r. sirkon, pr. o-sirkon-an, -ei, p.p. ko-sirkon-an.
- **Rob, to**: r. chôr, pr. o-chur-an, -ei, p.p. kô-chor-an.
- **Roll up, to**: r. maman, pr. o-memen-an, -ei, p.p. ka-maman-an.
- **Roof**: *use ad.* yim = *above*.
- **Room**, kô, pl. kôr. *Vide* **House**, *id.*
  *Space*, parô.
- **Root**, ĭghen, pl. ĭghīn.
- **Root out, to**: r. chit, pr. o-chit-an, -ei, p.p. ko-chit-an.
- **Rope**, perkeiy-oʻ, pl. perkeiy-i.
- **Rot, to**: r. nun, pr. o-nun-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nun-an.
  *Rotten*, taitagh, pl. taitagh-ei.
- **Round**, tenogh, pl. tenogh-ech.
- **Row, to set in a**: r. tăt, pr. o-tet-an, p.p. ka-tat-an.
- **Rub, to**: r. chich, pr. o-chich-an, -ei, p.p. ka-chich-an.
  *To rub 'off skin', or 'in ointment'*: r. nyer, pr. o-nyer-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nyer-an.
- **Rubbish**, pores, pures (lit. *goat's dung*).
- **Rump**, tong-o, pl. tong-oi.
- **Run, to**: r. rip, pr. o-rip-an, ei, p.p. ko-rip-an.
  *To run away from*: pr. o-rūp-oi-an, p.p. ko-rup-an.
  *I escape*, o-chôrt-a' kegh; (ii) r. tiak.
  *Runaway*, ki-rip-in, pl. ki-rip-ŭ.
- **Rupee**, rupia, pl. rupia-tin.
- **Rust**, piya'-karin (lit. *iron-dung*).

### S

- **Saliva**, ñgul (coll.).

- **Sand**, ñgaiyam (coll.).

- **Save, to**: r. ghet, pr. o-ghet-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ghet-an.

- **Say, to** (*cont.*):
  *To tell*: (i) r. mwô, pr. o-mwô-chin-an, p.p. kô-mwô-ch-an; (ii) r. len, pr. o-len-jin-an, p.p. ko-len-j-an.

- **Scar**, pérŭt, pl. perút-ai, perut-it.

- **Scarify, to**: r. ñgwar, pr. o-ñgwer-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgwar-an.
- **Scatter, to**: r. to, pr. o-to-soi-an, p.p. kô-to-i-an.
- **Scorch, to** (*e.g. meat*): r. pal, pr. o-pēl-an, p.p. ka-pāl-an.
  *To consume*: r. lach, pr. o-lech-an, -ei, p.p. ka-lach-an.
- **Scorpion**, tokol, pl. tokol-on-ut.
- **Scour, to**: r. sut, pr. o-sut-an, -ei, p.p. ka-sut-an.
- **Scout**, wot-in, pl. wot-ŭ.
- **Scowl at, to**: r. ñgwerial, pr. o-ñgweriel-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgwerial-an.
- **Scrape, to**: r. sip, pr. o-sīp-an, -ei, p.p. ko-sip-an; (ii) *Vide under* **Polish**.
  *To peel*: r. pôt, pr. o-pôt-an, -ei, p.p. kô-pôt-an; (ii) r. puch.

- **Scratch, to** (*as an itch*): r. ñgwar, pr. o-ñgwer-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgwar-an.
  *I scratch myself*, o-ñgwer-a' kegh.
  *To claw (once only), or to scratch as a hen*: r. kôl, pr. o-kul-an, -ei, p.p. kô-kôl-an.
- **Scrotum**, mela'-tērkel.
- **Sea** (*lake*),¹ wëiwëi, pl. wëiwëi-tin.
- **Search, to**: r. iyăr, pr. o-iyer-an, -ei, p.p. ka-iyar-an.
- **Season** (*wet*), peñgat (lit. *fresh grass*).
  (*dry*), kamei (lit. *hunger*).
- **Seat** (*stool for sitting*), (kip)-tepoñgmwen, pl. tepoñgmwen-tin.
  (*stool for resting neck*), ñgechĕr, ñgechēr.
- **Section** (*of warriors*), sebĭch, pl. sebĭch-is.
- **See, to**: pr. o-sow-iy-an, p.p. ka-sow-an; *irreg.*
- **Seed**, kesugh-on, pl. kesugh.
- **Seek, to**: r. iyar. *Vide* **Search**.
  *To desire*: r. mĕch, pr. o-mech-an, -ei, p.p. ka-mech-an.
- **Seize, to**: r. nam. *Vide* **Hold**, *id.*
  *To seize by force*: r. wôk, pr. o-wôk-in-an, p.p. kô-wôk-i-an.
- **Select, to**: r. kĕl, pr. o-kel-an, -ei, p.p. ka-kel-an.
- **Sell, to**: r. l, pr. ō-l-an, p.p. ka-l-an, imp. altena.
  *Seller*, ōl-in, pl. ol-ŭ.
- **Send, to**: (i) r. p, pr. o-p-an, p.p. kô-p-an; (ii) r. rañg, pr. o-roñg-an, p.p. ka-rañg-an.
  *I will send to him*, o-roñg-a ka nyende.
  *To send a person*: r. iyok, pr. o-iyok-an, -ei, p.p. ko-iyok-an.
  *To send away*: r. wan, pr. o-wen-an, ei, p.p. kô-wan-an.

> ¹ A lake is such a common feature of this part of Africa that one is surprised to find such utterly different words for it amongst neighbouring tribes, e.g. Suk *weiwei*, Turk. *em-baso*, Masai *en-aiposha*, En-jemusi *baringo*.

*(Page 138)*

- **Send, to** (*cont.*):
  *To send back*: (i) r. iyoit, pr. o-iyoit-oi-an, -ei, p.p. ko-iyoit-an; (ii) r. iyuk, pr. o-iyuk-un-an, p.p. ko-iyuk-u-an.
- **Sense**, ñgohom-et.
  *He has no sense*, me-tiñget-nye met.
- **Sentry, a**, rīp-in, pl. rīp-u.
- **Separate**, lōh, pl. loh-w-ech.
  *Apart*, kamas.
- **Separate fighters, to**: r. ghet, pr. o-ghet-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ghet-an.
- **Servant**, metuar-in, pl. metuar.
  *I serve a European*, o-igh-isi-a' ka Muzungu-yon.
- **Set, to**: r. ten, pr. o-ten-an, -ei, p.p. kô-ten-an.
  *To plant*: r. kotkot, pr. o-kotkot-an, -ei, p.p. kô-kotkot-an.
  *The sun has set*, ka-rēt Asis.
  *To set a (trap)*: r. tigh, pr. o-tigh-an (merap), kô-tigh-an.
  *I set fire to his house last night*, kô-ten-a' maʻ amena kô-nye.
  *To set in a line*: r. tat, pr. o-tet-an, -ei, p.p. ka-tat-an.
  *To set out (on a journey)*: r. ñget, pr. o-ñget-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñget-an.
  *To set up*: r. ghany, pr. o-ghony-it-an, p.p. ka-ghany-it-an; (ii) r. tur. *Vide* **Stand**.
- **Seven**, mūt-ngô-oghieñg.
- **Seventeen**, taman-ngô-mut-ngô-oghieñg.
- **Seventh**, pa-mut-ngô-oghieñg.
- **Seventy**, konom-ngô-tiptem; listam.
- **Sew, to**: r. nap, pr. o-nep-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nap-an.
  *To sew a patch on skins*: r. kĭn, pr. o-kin-an, -ei, p.p. kô-kin-an.
- **Shade, Shadow**, (i) ror, pl. rurwei; (ii) rur-wa, pl. rur-on-oi, rur-wei; (iii) kitondo, pl. kitondo-ei.
- **Shake, to**: r. sach, pr. o-sech-an, -ei, p.p. ka-sach-an.
  *To shake about (intr.)*: r. mwul, pr. o-mwulmwul-an, -ei, p.p. ka-mwulmwul-an.
  *To shake out*: r. part, pr. o-pert-oi-an, p.p. ka-part-an.
  *To shake milk*: r. sach, pr. o-sosoch-an, p.p. ka-sasach-an.
  *I shake myself*, o-pirpir-a' kegh.
- **Shame**, konyit.
- **Share, to**: r. par, pr. o-pôr-an, -ei, p.p. ka-pār-an.
  *A share*, karēran, pl. *id.*
- **Sharp**, ñgatip, pl. ñgatip-ech.
- **Sharpen, to**: r. let, pr. o-let-an, p.p. ka-let-an.
- **Shave**, r. ruak, pr. o-ruok-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ruak-an.
  *To pull out hairs*: r. pĭt, pr. o-pit-an, -ei, p.p. ko-pit-an.
- **She**, nyende. *Vide* **He**, *id.*
- **Sheath**, melot. *Vide* **Scabbard**.
  *Sheath-belt*, añgmwa-chuk.
- **Sheep**, kech, pl. kech-ir.
  *Ram*, makal, pl. mokol.
  *Castrated sheep*, setīm, pl. *id.*
  *Ewe*, nya-sakate, pl. cha-sakôti.
  *Lamb*, wawa, pl. wara.
- **Shell** (*tortoise*), kokech, pl. koke'-tin.
  *Husk*, mŭr-ion, pl. mŭr.
  *To husk*: r. sak, pr. o-sek-an, -ei, p.p. ka-sak-an.
- **Shepherd**, maso-won, pl. masō.
- **Shield**, loñg-ô, pl. loñg-oi.
  *Rib of shield*, ket-pa-loñg-ô.
  *Handle of shield*, apapai, pl. apapai-tin.
  *Shin*, kŏrŏk, pl. kōrūk.
- **Shine**: *The sun shines*, Riol-ei Asis.
- **Shiver, to**: r. potan, pr. o-poten-an, -ei, p.p. kô-potan-an.
- **Shoot, to** (*with arrows*): r. wut, pr. o-wut-an, -ei, p.p. ko-wut-an.

- **Shoot, to** (*cont.*):

- **Shorten, to**: rīñg, pr. o-riñg-an, -ei, p.p. ka-riñg-an.
- **Shoulder** (*of men or animals*), leiy-oʻ, pl. leiy-us.
- **Shout, to**: r. tegh, pr. o-tegh-an, -ei, p.p. ka-tegh-an; (ii) r. wut.
  *To call*: kūr, pr. o-kur-an, -ei, p.p. ko-kur-an.
- **Show to, to**: r. pôr, pr. o-pôr-chi-n-an, p.p. kô-pôr-ch-an.

- **Shudder, to**: r. potan. *Vide* **Shake**.

  *Sickness (cattle)*, semiyu.

  (*of house*), komas, pl. *id.*
  (*of meat, &c.*), kareran, pl. *id.*
  *Side by side*, komas komas.
  *One-sided (adj.)*, yimote, pl. yimot-ene.
- **Sift grain, to** (*by shaking*): r. sak, pr. o-sek-an, -ei, p.p. ka-sak-an.
  (*by tossing*): r. kat, pr. o-ket-an, -ei, p.p. ka-kat-an.
- **Silent, to be**: r. sīs, pr. o-sis-an, p.p. ka-sis-an.
  *Silent person*, sīs-in, pl. sis-ŭ.
  *Silently*, aiyisis.
- **Sin, to**: r. nyar, pr. o-nyôr-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nyar-an.

- **Sing, to**: r. tūm, pr. o-tum-an, -ei, p.p. ko-tum-an.
- **Sink, to** (*intrans.*): r. lĭt, pr. o-lit-a' (pogh), p.p. ko-lit-an.

- **Sister**, chep-te-nja, pl. tipi-cha.
- **Sister-in-law** (*wife's sister*), *sister's husband*, *id.*, ora, pl. ora-tin.

- **Sit, to**: r. tep, pr. o-tep-oi-an, p.p. kô-tep-an.

- **Six**, mūt-ngô-okoñgô.
- **Sixteen**, taman-ngô-mut-ngô-okoñgô.

- **Slap, to**: r. rapach, pr. o-ropoch-an, p.p. ka-rapach-an.
- **Slash, to**: r. ter, pr. o-ter-an, ei, p.p. kô-ter-an.
- **Slaughter, to** (*of an animal or of wholesale murder*): r. par, p.p. o-per-an, p.p. ka-par-an.

- **Sleek**, akwagh, pl. akwŏgh-ech.

  *To be sleepy*: r. nuch, pr. o-nuch-an, -ei, p.p. ko-nuch-an.

  pr. o-perper-oi-an, p.p. ka-perper-an.

- **Sole of foot**, ōrī-kel.
- **Some**, anga, pl. w-alak.
  *Some one*, chi-anga, kam-ne.
  *Something*, ki-t-anga.

- **Soot**, kiripos, pl. kiripos-tin.

- **is Soughing** (*of wind*), kut-ei yamat.

  *He stole from a man*, ka-chôr a chi.

### T

- **Tail**, sarôr, pl. saror-ai.
  *Tail of sheep*, tūr, pl. tur-o.
  *Tail-hairs*, adērdĭü, pl. adērdīü-tin.
- **Tailor**, nep-in, pl. nep-ŭ.
- **Take, to**: r. cheñg, pr. o-cheñg-an, -ei, p.p. ka-cheñg-an.
  *To receive*: r. tāch, pr. o-tôch-an, -ei, p.p. ka-tach-an.
  *To take on a journey*: r. mĕt, pr. o-met-an, -ei, p.p. ka-met-an.
  *To take a walk*: r. weset, pr. o-weset-oi-an, p.p. ko-weset-an.
  *To take by force*: r. sar, pr. o-ser-oi-an, p.p. ka-sar-an.
  *Take care!* Rŏs-a!
  *To take care of*: r. rīp, pr. o-rīp-an, -ei, p.p. ko-rip-an.
  *To take down*: r. tiar, pr. o-tiar-un-an, p.p. kô-tiar-u-an.
  *To take leave*: r. rôt, pr. o-rôt-an, p.p. ko-rôt-an.
  *To take off (clothes)*: r. ñgūs, pr. o-ñgus-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgus-an.
  *To take off the fire*: r. sĭt, pr. o-sit-an, -ei, p.p. ko-sit-an.
  *To take up a load*: r. sit. *Vide supra*.
  *To take out*: r. ñgūs. *Vide supra*.
  *To hold*: r. nam, pr. o-nem-an, -ei, p.p. ka-nam-an.
  *To take up with one hand*: r. kŭmit, pr. o-kumit-an, p.p. ka-kumit-an.
  *To take up with both hands*: r. ram, pr. o-rem-an, p.p. ka-ram-an.
- **Tale**, kikôren, pl. *id.*
- **Talk, to**: r. ñgalan, pr. o-ñgal-ôn-an, -ei, p.p. ka-ñgalan-an.
  *To converse, q.v.*: (i) r. kôr, pr. o-kor-an, -ei, p.p. kôkôr-an; (ii) r. chiren.
  *To talk behind back*: r. chām, pr. o-chôm-an, p.p. ka-chām-an.

  *Circumcised*, muren, pl. *id.*

### Z

- **Zebra**, che-marmar, pl. che-mŏr-mŏr.

## APPENDIX

*(Page 150)*

The Turkana influence on Suk may be illustrated by the following comparative table:—

| *English.* | *Turkana.*¹ | *Suk.* |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Pond | s. nya-tapor / pl. ñga-taparin | s. takar. / pl. takar-ai. |
| herdsman | s. nye-yokon | verbal r. iyakwiy, to herd. |
| tree | s. nye-kitoi / pl. ñgi-kito | ket [pl. *id.*]. |
| donkey | s. nye-sikirya / pl. ñgi-sikirya | s. sigiri-oʻ. / pl. sigir-ai. |
| bee | s. nya-o / pl. ñga-awa | cf. wawa sakam, larvae of bees. |
| milk | ñga-kile | keleloʻ, curdled milk. |
| stones for cooking-pot | s. nya-muro / pl. ñga-mur | s. nimur-ion. / pl. nimur. |
| ford, ferry | s. nye-doket / pl. ñgi-doketa | s. adoghet. / pl. adoghet-in. |
| bed | s. nya-peryet / pl. ñga-peryeto | s. pareiy. / pl. pareiy-us. |
| giraffe | s. nye-kori / pl. ñgi-korio | s. akori. / pl. akori-tin. |
| horse | s. nye-ñgole / pl. ñga-ñgolei | s. añgoli. / pl. añgoli-tin. |
| smoke | apuru | cf. kapuret, mist. |
| song | edoñga | cf. kedoñgô, a musical dance. |
| spoon | akholopoch | s. akolopoin. / pl. akolopoin-tin. |
| house | s. nya-khai | s. kai. |
| hump (of ox or camel) | s. nya-rug | s. wuk. |
| hunger | s. nya-khamir | s. kamei. |
| fish | s. ekhol-a | s. koögh-wa. |
| detachment | s. asepich | s. sebech. / pl. sebech-is. |
| coward | s. ekuryana / pl. ñga-kuryamakh | cf. adj. cowardly, s. nyakor. / pl. nyakor-ech. |

> ¹ From papers lent to me by Mr. A. C. Hollis.

*(Page 151)*

| *English.* | *Turkana.* | *Suk.* |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| chair | ñgi-cholongo | ñgecher. |
| bone | s. akhoit / pl. nya-koyo | s. kôwô. / pl. kô. |
| buffaloes | pl. ñga-sowa | s. soy. / pl. soy-en. |
| animal | s. etyañg / pl. nye-tyañga | s. tiony. / pl. tiong-en. |
| bank of a river | s. ekwaye / pl. nge-kwa | s. endakwagh. / pl. endakwai'-tin. |

Some Turkana numerals are occasionally used. *Vide* p. 67.

#### NOTES ON MAP I (KERIO SUK COUNTRY):
1. *Cultivation at foot of Elgeyo Escarpment from Ndo to Merich.*
2. *Villages, not being permanent, are not marked.*
3. *Easy flat walking from Cheb-Karat to Kerio Post.*
4. *No permanent water in plains besides Rivers Kerio, Kerūt, Cheb-Karat (by digging) and Akuiloʻ, but during rains water everywhere in holes.*
5. *All journeys marked thus `---` reach water after a maximum march of 6 hours except route from Kerio to Kerūt between Masol and Laterok, which requires 8 hours without a halt.*
6. *Elephants do not go north of Ngabotoʻ or south of Chep-tulel.*
